### Category 4: Time, Entropy & Cosmology

#### `QU-18` — Nature of Time: Block Universe vs. Real Becoming
- **Category:** Time, Entropy & Cosmology
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Timeless Block Universe` | `Emergent Thermodynamic Time` | `Time Is Fundamentally Real`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Does relativity plus the timeless Wheeler–DeWitt equation abolish time at the fundamental level, or is the flow of time the one feature of reality physics must never explain away?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Timeless Block Universe:* Relativity of simultaneity and canonical quantum gravity point to a timeless configuration space where "now" is a local relational illusion (Barbour).
  - *Emergent Thermodynamic Time:* Microscopic laws have no fundamental time parameter; time's flow and direction emerge statistically from coarse-graining and entanglement (Rovelli).
  - *Time Is Fundamentally Real:* Treating time as an illusion turns laws into timeless mysteries; taking real becoming seriously lets even physical laws evolve (Smolin & Unger).
- **Primary citations:** B. S. DeWitt (1967), *Phys. Rev.*; J. Barbour (1999), *The End of Time*; C. Rovelli (2018), *The Order of Time*; L. Smolin (2013), *Time Reborn*.

#### `QU-19` — Explaining the Thermodynamic Arrow of Time
- **Category:** Time, Entropy & Cosmology
- **Geometry:** `triangle`
- **Poles:** `Past Hypothesis (Low-Entropy Start)` | `Gravitational / Dynamical Complexity` | `Time-Symmetric Two-Headed Cosmos`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Since fundamental microscopic laws are virtually time-symmetric, why did entropy start astonishingly low at the Big Bang?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Past Hypothesis (Low-Entropy Start):* The early universe simply had a special low-entropy boundary condition (e.g., Penrose's vanishing Weyl curvature), which requires a fundamental law of initial conditions.
  - *Gravitational / Dynamical Complexity:* Unbounded gravitational systems have no maximum-entropy equilibrium; expansion and gravitational clumping naturally create arrows of complexity without special fine-tuning.
  - *Time-Symmetric Two-Headed Cosmos:* The universe as a whole may be time-symmetric around a low-entropy bounce or Janus point, with thermodynamic arrows pointing outward in both directions.
- **Primary citations:** L. Boltzmann (1895); R. Penrose (1979), *General Relativity: An Einstein Centenary Survey*; D. Z. Albert (2000), *Time and Chance*; S. M. Carroll & J. Chen (2004); J. Barbour et al. (2014).

#### `QU-20` — Cosmic Inflation vs. Bouncing & Cyclic Cosmologies
- **Category:** Time, Entropy & Cosmology
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Inflation Is Established Pillar` | `Inflation Promising Yet Unfinished` | `Bouncing / Cyclic Alternatives Preferable`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Has cosmic inflation been vindicated by precision CMB measurements, or do eternal inflation, the measure problem, and non-detection of primordial gravitational waves favor bouncing alternatives?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Inflation Is Established Pillar:* Flatness, super-horizon acoustic correlations, and the slightly red-tilted scalar spectrum ($n_s \approx 0.965$) are stunning empirical confirmations of inflation.
  - *Inflation Promising Yet Unfinished:* Inflation fits CMB data extraordinarily well, yet still needs a clean UV embedding, initial-condition resolution, and $B$-mode tensor detection.
  - *Bouncing / Cyclic Alternatives Preferable:* Eternal inflation makes inflation unfalsifiable by producing every possible universe; ekpyrotic/bouncing models avoid both singularities and multiverses.
- **Primary citations:** A. H. Guth (1981), *Phys. Rev. D*; A. D. Linde (1982); P. J. Steinhardt & N. Turok (2002), *Science*; A. Ijjas, P. J. Steinhardt & A. Loeb (2013, 2017) & Guth et al. (2017) *Sci. Am.* debate.

#### `QU-21` — Multiverse & Anthropic Selection
- **Category:** Time, Entropy & Cosmology
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Unscientific Surrender` | `Cautious Environmental Selection` | `Inevitable Physical Landscape`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Is invoking a vast multiverse of pocket universes to explain the tiny cosmological constant a legitimate Copernican step or the abandonment of physics?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Unscientific Surrender:* Unobservable parallel universes explain anything and predict nothing; invoking them stops physicists from searching for real dynamical explanations.
  - *Cautious Environmental Selection:* Like Earth's distance from the Sun, some parameters (such as $\Lambda$) may genuinely be environmental—use anthropics sparingly and only with sharp statistical bounds.
  - *Inevitable Physical Landscape:* Eternal inflation plus the $10^{500}$ vacua of string theory naturally generate a physical multiverse; refusing to follow our best theories where they lead is dogma.
- **Primary citations:** S. Weinberg (1987), *Phys. Rev. Lett.*; L. Susskind (2003), "The Anthropic Landscape of String Theory"; G. F. R. Ellis (2011), *Sci. Am.*

#### `QU-22` — Dark Matter vs. Modified Gravity
- **Category:** Time, Entropy & Cosmology
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Particle / Compact Dark Matter Wins` | `Hybrid Emergent / Superfluid DM` | `Modified Gravity Deserves Equal Shot`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Do galactic rotation curves, gravitational lensing, and CMB acoustic peaks prove the existence of cold dark matter, or does the tight radial acceleration relation hint at modified gravity (MOND)?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Particle / Compact Dark Matter Wins:* The CMB power spectrum, large-scale structure, and Bullet Cluster lensing overwhelmingly favor non-baryonic dark matter over any known gravity modification.
  - *Hybrid Emergent / Superfluid DM:* Perhaps dark matter behaves like CDM on cosmic scales while forming a superfluid or interacting medium that mimics MOND inside galaxies.
  - *Modified Gravity Deserves Equal Shot:* Decades of null WIMP searches and the uncanny predictive accuracy of Milgrom's $a_0$ scale across galaxies demand taking modified gravity seriously.
- **Primary citations:** V. C. Rubin & W. K. Ford (1970); M. Milgrom (1983), *ApJ*; D. Clowe et al. (2006), *ApJL*; S. S. McGaugh, F. Lelli & J. M. Schombert (2016), *Phys. Rev. Lett.*

#### `QU-23` — The Cosmological Constant Problem ($\Lambda$)
- **Category:** Time, Entropy & Cosmology
- **Geometry:** `triangle`
- **Poles:** `Anthropic Landscape Selection` | `Dynamical Screening / Dark Energy` | `QFT Vacuum-Energy Calculation Is Misframed`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Why is the observed dark-energy density up to 120 orders of magnitude smaller than naive quantum-field-theory zero-point estimates?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Anthropic Landscape Selection:* Weinberg's 1987 anthropic prediction came within an order of magnitude of the observed $\Lambda$—still the only successful quantitative explanation.
  - *Dynamical Screening / Dark Energy:* A dynamical quintessence field, unimodular gravity, or infrared gravitational backreaction screens vacuum energy naturally.
  - *QFT Vacuum-Energy Calculation Is Misframed:* $\Lambda$ is simply a renormalized classical coupling constant in Einstein's equation; naive flat-space UV cutoffs manufacture a fake crisis (Bianchi & Rovelli).
- **Primary citations:** S. Weinberg (1989), *Rev. Mod. Phys.*; E. Bianchi & C. Rovelli (2010), "Why All These Prejudices Against a Constant?," *arXiv:1002.3966*.

---

### Category 5: Reductionism, Emergence & Complexity

#### `QU-24` — Weinberg's Reductionism vs. Anderson's "More Is Different"
- **Category:** Reductionism, Emergence & Complexity
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Arrows of Explanation Point Down` | `Multi-Scale Co-Equal Autonomy` | `Emergent Organizing Laws Are Fundamental`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Is high-energy particle physics uniquely "fundamental" while condensed-matter and statistical physics are derivative, or are emergent collective principles equally fundamental laws of nature?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Arrows of Explanation Point Down:* Ultimately, why a superconductor works traces down to QED and the Standard Model, whereas no condensed-matter principle explains the Standard Model (Weinberg).
  - *Multi-Scale Co-Equal Autonomy:* Microphysics constrains what is possible, yet higher-scale universality classes are robust and autonomous from microscopic details.
  - *Emergent Organizing Laws Are Fundamental:* Even relativity, gauge fields, and chiral fermions may themselves be emergent collective excitations of an underlying quantum substrate (Anderson, Laughlin, Wen).
- **Primary citations:** P. W. Anderson (1972), "More Is Different," *Science*; S. Weinberg (1987, 1993); R. B. Laughlin & D. Pines (2000), "The Theory of Everything," *PNAS*.

#### `QU-25` — Information as Ontological Bedrock ("It from Bit")
- **Category:** Reductionism, Emergence & Complexity
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Matter & Fields Are Primary` | `Matter and Information Are Dual` | `Quantum Information Is Ultimate Substrate`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Is information merely an abstract measure of physical states, or did John Archibald Wheeler foresee rightly that every physical "it" derives from quantum "bits"?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Matter & Fields Are Primary:* Information is always *about* something physical; treating abstract bits as ontology confuses the map with the territory.
  - *Matter and Information Are Dual:* Landauer's principle, black-hole thermodynamics, and entanglement entropy show physical dynamics and information are two inseparable faces of reality.
  - *Quantum Information Is Ultimate Substrate:* Reconstructing quantum theory and spacetime geometry from information-theoretic axioms reveals reality as fundamentally informational.
- **Primary citations:** J. A. Wheeler (1990), "Information, Physics, Quantum: The Search for Links"; D. Deutsch (1997); C. G. Timpson (2013), *Quantum Information Theory and the Foundations of Quantum Mechanics*.

#### `QU-26` — Philosophical Meaning of the Renormalization Group
- **Category:** Reductionism, Emergence & Complexity
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Technical Recipe for Removing Infinities` | `Scale-Flow of Effective Descriptions` | `Deepest Law of Universality & Autonomy`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Is Wilsonian renormalization merely a clever mathematical broom for UV divergences, or the deepest conceptual explanation for why physics works at all across scales?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Technical Recipe for Removing Infinities:* Renormalization fixes perturbative singularities cleanly, though a truly finite fundamental theory shouldn't need subtraction tricks.
  - *Scale-Flow of Effective Descriptions:* RG systematically tracks how couplings run with energy scale, telling us which operators matter in any experimental window.
  - *Deepest Law of Universality & Autonomy:* RG fixed points explain why vastly different microscopic systems share identical critical exponents—making autonomous macroscopic science possible.
- **Primary citations:** K. G. Wilson (1975), *Rev. Mod. Phys.*; R. W. Batterman (2002), *The Devil in the Details*; S. Weinberg (1995).

#### `QU-27` — Symmetry Breaking vs. Topological Order
- **Category:** Reductionism, Emergence & Complexity
- **Geometry:** `linear-sliders`
- **Poles:** `Exact Symmetry & Spontaneous Breaking` | `Long-Range Entanglement & Topological Order`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** How heavily do you lean on Landau's symmetry-breaking paradigm versus modern topological order and generalized (higher-form/non-invertible) symmetries?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Exact Symmetry & Spontaneous Breaking:* Symmetry principles and their spontaneous breaking (Goldstone, Higgs) remain the supreme organizing engine of physics.
  - *Long-Range Entanglement & Topological Order:* Fractional quantum Hall states, spin liquids, and generalized symmetries reveal rich quantum phases completely invisible to local order parameters.
- **Primary citations:** L. D. Landau (1937); P. W. Anderson (1963); X.-G. Wen (2004), *Quantum Field Theory of Many-Body Systems*; D. Gaiotto et al. (2015), *JHEP*.

---

### Category 6: Epistemology & Practice of Theoretical Physics

#### `QU-28` — Non-Empirical Theory Assessment vs. Strict Falsifiability
- **Category:** Epistemology & Practice of Theoretical Physics
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Strict Popperian Falsifiability` | `Cautious Heuristic Priors` | `Bayesian Non-Empirical Confirmation`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** When experiments cannot yet test Planck-scale theories, can lack of alternatives, unexpected internal coherence, and meta-inductive track records legitimately raise confidence in a theory?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Strict Popperian Falsifiability:* Redefining science to accept untestable theories breaks a 400-year safeguard against self-delusion (Ellis & Silk).
  - *Cautious Heuristic Priors:* Mathematical consistency and unification legitimately guide *which* theories to study—just never call them confirmed before empirical proof.
  - *Bayesian Non-Empirical Confirmation:* Scientists have always updated credence using theoretical coherence and no-alternatives arguments; formalizing this honestly matches real scientific practice (Dawid).
- **Primary citations:** K. Popper (1959); R. Dawid (2013), *String Theory and the Scientific Method*; G. F. R. Ellis & J. Silk (2014), "Scientific Method: Defend the Integrity of Physics," *Nature*.

#### `QU-29` — Formal Mathematical Theory vs. Data-Driven Phenomenology
- **Category:** Epistemology & Practice of Theoretical Physics
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Close-to-Data Phenomenology` | `Ambidextrous Bridge-Builder` | `Pure Formal Structure`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Where does a theoretical physicist make the highest-impact contribution—building models tightly coupled to upcoming experiments, or exploring pure formal structures of QFT and gravity?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Close-to-Data Phenomenology:* Theory stays alive only when anchored to colliders, telescopes, and tabletop probes; build models experimentalists can actually test next year.
  - *Ambidextrous Bridge-Builder:* Translate formal insights (dualities, EFT bounds, symmetry tools) into sharp experimental signatures and vice versa.
  - *Pure Formal Structure:* Exploring the mathematical bones of QFT, dualities, and quantum gravity uncovers tools that transform all of physics decades later.
- **Primary citations:** Practitioner subdisciplinary divide (HEP-ph vs. HEP-th cultures); F. Dyson (2009); S. Weinberg (1995).

#### `QU-30` — Mathematical Rigor in Quantum Field Theory
- **Category:** Epistemology & Practice of Theoretical Physics
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Physical Path-Integral Intuition` | `Selective Rigorous Anchors` | `Axiomatic & Constructive QFT Mandate`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Physicists compute QED to twelve decimal places using path integrals mathematicians still cannot define rigorously in 4D; how much does that bother you?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Physical Path-Integral Intuition:* Waiting for mathematicians to formalize every integral would have stalled 20th-century physics; physical consistency and experiment are the real referees.
  - *Selective Rigorous Anchors:* Use heuristic path integrals to explore freely, but lean on axiomatic theorems (CPT, spin-statistics, algebraic QFT) when subtle paradoxes strike.
  - *Axiomatic & Constructive QFT Mandate:* Unrigorous formalisms hide conceptual blunders (like Haag's theorem and UV/IR ambiguities); constructing interacting 4D Yang–Mills rigorously is a premier goal of physics.
- **Primary citations:** R. F. Streater & A. S. Wightman (1964), *PCT, Spin and Statistics, and All That*; J. Glimm & A. Jaffe (1987), *Quantum Physics: A Functional Integral Point of View*; Clay Millennium Yang–Mills Problem (Jaffe & Witten 2000).

#### `QU-31` — Toy Models vs. Realistic Complexity & Analytic vs. Symbolic Tools
- **Category:** Epistemology & Practice of Theoretical Physics
- **Geometry:** `xy-switchbox`
- **Poles:** `X: Solvable Toy Models (Ising, SYK, 2D CFT) ↔ Realistic Messy Systems` | `Y: Pen-and-Paper Analytical ↔ Computer Algebra & Numerical Assist`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** How do you prefer to crack a hard theoretical physics problem?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Solvable Toy Models (X-Low):* Strip away realistic clutter until an exactly solvable core (Ising, SYK, $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM) exposes the pure mechanism.
  - *Realistic Messy Systems (X-High):* Toy models often smuggle in artificial symmetries; confront real 3+1D, non-supersymmetric, disorder-laden systems directly.
  - *Pen-and-Paper Analytical (Y-Low):* Closed-form derivations, asymptotics, and symmetry arguments build intuition no computer printout can replace.
  - *Computer Algebra & Numerical Assist (Y-High):* Symbolic algebra, lattice checks, and numerical bootstrap push past human algebraic bottlenecks into uncharted non-perturbative regimes.
- **Primary citations:** S. Sachdev & J. Ye (1993); A. Kitaev (2015) SYK model; D. Poland et al. (2019) numerical conformal bootstrap.

#### `QU-32` — Expectation of Grand Unification
- **Category:** Epistemology & Practice of Theoretical Physics
- **Geometry:** `monopole-slider`
- **Poles:** `Belief in a Single Unified Theory of All Forces`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** How strongly do you expect strong, electroweak, and gravitational interactions to merge into one single, simple unified mathematical structure?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Belief in a Single Unified Theory of All Forces:* Running gauge couplings, anomaly cancellation across generations, and the history from Maxwell to Weinberg–Salam point unmistakably toward ultimate unification.
- **Primary citations:** H. Georgi & S. L. Glashow (1974), *Phys. Rev. Lett.*; S. Weinberg (1993), *Dreams of a Final Theory*.

#### `QU-33` — Preferred Theoretical Aesthetic
- **Category:** Epistemology & Practice of Theoretical Physics
- **Geometry:** `star-radar`
- **Poles:** `Symmetry & Invariance` | `Analytical Solvability` | `Minimal Free Parameters` | `Unexpected Cross-Domain Duality` | `Sharp Experimental Predictivity`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** What gives you the deepest intellectual thrill in a theoretical paper?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Symmetry & Invariance:* Deriving an entire force law or particle multiplet from a single crisp invariance principle.
  - *Analytical Solvability:* Finding an exact closed-form solution, integrability structure, or exact renormalization flow.
  - *Minimal Free Parameters:* Explaining disparate constants and ratios from pure structure with zero arbitrary tuning knobs.
  - *Unexpected Cross-Domain Duality:* Discovering that strong coupling in one theory equals weak coupling in an utterly different geometry or condensed-matter system.
  - *Sharp Experimental Predictivity:* Sticking your neck out with a clean, risky numerical prediction that an experiment can decisively confirm or kill.
- **Primary citations:** P. A. M. Dirac (1963); C. N. Yang (1980), *Phys. Today*; S. Weinberg (1993).

#### `QU-34` — Role of Paradoxes & Thought Experiments
- **Category:** Epistemology & Practice of Theoretical Physics
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Wait for Experimental Anomalies` | `Targeted Conceptual Checks` | `Paradoxes Drive Physics Revolutions`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Should theorists stay grounded in empirical discrepancies, or chase pure thought-experiment paradoxes (EPR, black-hole firewalls, Wigner's friend) as engines of revolution?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Wait for Experimental Anomalies:* Thought experiments without experimental anchors easily chase artifacts of idealized approximations.
  - *Targeted Conceptual Checks:* Use thought experiments to stress-test consistency of established theories, without confusing paradox-chasing for empirical discovery.
  - *Paradoxes Drive Physics Revolutions:* Chasing light beams, falling elevators, EPR, and black-hole evaporation sparked the greatest conceptual revolutions in physics.
- **Primary citations:** A. Einstein (1905–1935); S. W. Hawking (1976); J. Polchinski (2017), "Memories of a Theoretical Physicist."

#### `QU-35` — Research Strategy: Mainstream Program vs. Independent Contrarian
- **Category:** Epistemology & Practice of Theoretical Physics
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Advance Dominant Mainstream Program` | `Mainstream Core + Contrarian Bets` | `Independent Unorthodox Foundations`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** In speculative fundamental theory, is it wiser to contribute to the richest, best-tested collective research program, or strike out independently against groupthink?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Advance Dominant Mainstream Program:* Brilliant communities converge on dominant frameworks for good technical reasons; cumulative teamwork beats isolated reinventing of the wheel.
  - *Mainstream Core + Contrarian Bets:* Master the strongest mainstream tools while keeping an independent mind ready to pivot when assumptions crack.
  - *Independent Unorthodox Foundations:* When a field goes decades without experimental confirmation, intellectual diversity and fearless outsider programs are essential to escape local minima.
- **Primary citations:** L. Smolin (2006), *The Trouble with Physics*; P. Woit (2006), *Not Even Wrong*; J. Polchinski (2007) review responses.

## 4. Canonical Archetypes (7 Archetypes)

### 1. The String-Holographic Unifier
- **Summary:** Pursues ultimate UV unification through string theory, dualities, AdS/CFT holography, quantum-extremal islands, and emergent entanglement geometry.
- **Primary Thinkers & Literature:** E. Witten, J. M. Maldacena (1998), J. Polchinski (1998), L. Susskind (2003), N. Arkani-Hamed.
- **Stance Profile:**
  - **Anchors High:** `QU-07` (*String Theory & Holography*), `QU-08` (*Gravity Must Be Fully Quantum*), `QU-09` (*Universal Principle of Real Quantum Gravity*), `QU-10` (*Unitary Page Curve via Quantum Extremal Islands*), `QU-11` (*Spacetime Emerges from Entanglement*), `QU-14` (*Realized Only at High / Planck Scales* or *Still Essential Symmetry*), `QU-16` (*EFT + Swampland UV–IR Constraints* / *Only Complete UV Theory*), `QU-17` (*Deep Elegance Reliably Guides Discovery*), `QU-21` (*Inevitable Physical Landscape* or *Cautious Environmental Selection*), `QU-28` (*Bayesian Non-Empirical Confirmation*), `QU-29` (*Pure Formal Structure*), `QU-32` (High *Belief in Unified Theory*), `QU-33` (*Unexpected Cross-Domain Duality*).
  - **Anchors Low:** `QU-07` (*Loop Quantum Gravity*), `QU-28` (*Strict Popperian Falsifiability*), `QU-35` (*Independent Unorthodox Foundations*).

### 2. The Background-Independent Relativist
- **Summary:** Takes Einstein's diffeomorphism invariance and background independence literally, quantizing spacetime geometry directly without extra dimensions or speculative multiverses.
- **Primary Thinkers & Literature:** C. Rovelli (1996, 2004, 2018), L. Smolin (2006, 2013), A. Ashtekar, E. Bianchi.
- **Stance Profile:**
  - **Anchors High:** `QU-02` (*Relational & Perspectival State* for Rovelli wing), `QU-07` (*Loop Quantum Gravity & Spin Foams*), `QU-08` (*Gravity Must Be Fully Quantum*), `QU-09` (*Mathematical Toy in Wrong Spacetime*), `QU-11` (*Quantum-Geometric Granularity*), `QU-12` (High *Strict Non-Perturbative Background Independence*), `QU-18` (*Emergent Thermodynamic Time* [Rovelli] or *Time Is Fundamentally Real* [Smolin]), `QU-21` (*Unscientific Surrender*), `QU-23` (*QFT Vacuum-Energy Calculation Is Misframed*), `QU-35` (*Independent Unorthodox Foundations*).
  - **Anchors Low:** `QU-07` (*String Theory & Holography*), `QU-14` (*Still Essential Symmetry*), `QU-21` (*Inevitable Physical Landscape*).

### 3. The Everettian Wavefunction Realist
- **Summary:** Treats the universal quantum state evolving strictly by unitary dynamics as literal physical reality—rejecting collapse postulates, hidden variables, and observer mysticism.
- **Primary Thinkers & Literature:** H. Everett III (1957), D. Deutsch (1997), D. Wallace (2012), S. M. Carroll (2019).
- **Stance Profile:**
  - **Anchors High:** `QU-01` (*Everettian Many-Worlds*), `QU-02` (*Strictly ψ-Ontic Physical Reality*), `QU-03` (*Drop Single Outcomes (Many-Worlds)*), `QU-04` (*Foundational Clarity Is Primary*), `QU-05` (*Explains Basis, Not Definite Outcomes* — completed by Everett branching), `QU-08` (*Gravity Must Be Fully Quantum*), `QU-11` (*Spacetime Emerges from Entanglement*), `QU-19` (*Past Hypothesis* or *Time-Symmetric Two-Headed Cosmos*), `QU-24` (*Multi-Scale Co-Equal Autonomy* / poetic naturalism), `QU-34` (*Paradoxes Drive Physics Revolutions*).
  - **Anchors Low:** `QU-01` (*Copenhagen & QBism*), `QU-02` (*Strictly ψ-Epistemic Information*), `QU-04` (*Operational Pragmatism First*).

### 4. The Quantum-Information & QBist Pragmatist
- **Summary:** Strips away metaphysical wavefunction-as-substance pictures, treating quantum mechanics as an operational, agent-centered or information-theoretic law of interaction.
- **Primary Thinkers & Literature:** N. Bohr (1935), J. A. Wheeler (1990), C. A. Fuchs & R. Schack (2013), Č. Brukner (2018), A. Peres.
- **Stance Profile:**
  - **Anchors High:** `QU-01` (*Copenhagen & QBism*), `QU-02` (*Strictly ψ-Epistemic Information* or *Relational & Perspectival State*), `QU-03` (*Abandon Pre-Existing Realism*), `QU-06` (*Facts Are Strictly Observer-Relative*), `QU-25` (*Quantum Information Is Ultimate Substrate* / *Matter and Information Are Dual*).
  - **Anchors Low:** `QU-01` (*Bohmian Pilot-Wave* & *Everettian Many-Worlds*), `QU-02` (*Strictly ψ-Ontic Physical Reality*), `QU-06` (*Single Objective Reality of Facts*).

### 5. The Bellian & Bohmian Realist
- **Summary:** Demands a clear, observer-free physical ontology ("local beables") in 3D space—accepting non-locality honestly rather than tolerating vague measurement talk or branching worlds.
- **Primary Thinkers & Literature:** J. S. Bell (1964, 1990), D. Bohm (1952), T. Maudlin (2019), S. Goldstein, R. Tumulka.
- **Stance Profile:**
  - **Anchors High:** `QU-01` (*Bohmian Pilot-Wave* or *Objective Collapse*), `QU-02` (*Strictly ψ-Ontic Physical Reality*), `QU-03` (*Accept Non-Locality*), `QU-04` (*Foundational Clarity Is Primary*), `QU-05` (*Leaves Core Problem Untouched*), `QU-06` (*Single Objective Reality of Facts*), `QU-18` (*Time Is Fundamentally Real* / primitive dynamical foliation), `QU-30` (*Axiomatic & Constructive QFT Mandate* / clear ontology).
  - **Anchors Low:** `QU-01` (*Copenhagen & QBism*), `QU-04` (*Operational Pragmatism First*), `QU-05` (*Solves It For All Practical Purposes*), `QU-06` (*Facts Are Strictly Observer-Relative*).

### 6. The Andersonian Emergentist & EFT Realist
- **Summary:** Sees physics as a hierarchy of autonomous, RG-protected effective worlds where collective emergence and topological order are just as fundamental as Planck-scale microphysics.
- **Primary Thinkers & Literature:** P. W. Anderson (1972), K. G. Wilson (1975), R. B. Laughlin & D. Pines (2000), X.-G. Wen (2004), H. Georgi (1993).
- **Stance Profile:**
  - **Anchors High:** `QU-16` (*EFT Is All Empirical Physics Needs*), `QU-24` (*Emergent Organizing Laws Are Fundamental* / *Multi-Scale Co-Equal Autonomy*), `QU-26` (*Deepest Law of Universality & Autonomy*), `QU-27` (High on both *Spontaneous Breaking* and *Long-Range Entanglement & Topological Order*), `QU-31` (*Solvable Toy Models* + *Realistic Messy Systems* balanced).
  - **Anchors Low:** `QU-16` (*Only Complete UV Theory Satisfies*), `QU-24` (*Arrows of Explanation Point Down*), `QU-32` (Low/moderate *Belief in Single Unified Theory* as sole goal).

### 7. The Empirical Phenomenologist & Anti-Speculation Skeptic
- **Summary:** Grounds theoretical physics ruthlessly in empirical falsifiability, LHC/astrophysical data, and testable models—pushing back against naturalness dogma, untestable landscapes, and beauty-driven speculation.
- **Primary Thinkers & Literature:** P. Woit (2006), S. Hossenfelder (2018), G. F. R. Ellis & J. Silk (2014), A. Ijjas & P. J. Steinhardt (2013–2017), phenomenology community.
- **Stance Profile:**
  - **Anchors High:** `QU-08` (*Gravity May Remain Classical* — test experimentally!), `QU-13` (*Abandon Naturalness Bias*), `QU-14` (*Beautiful Failed Hypothesis for Nature*), `QU-16` (*EFT Is All Empirical Physics Needs*), `QU-17` (*Beauty Actively Misleads Physics*), `QU-20` (*Bouncing / Cyclic Alternatives Preferable* or *Inflation Needs Tighter Foundations*), `QU-21` (*Unscientific Surrender*), `QU-22` (*Modified Gravity Deserves Equal Shot* or data-driven open mind), `QU-28` (*Strict Popperian Falsifiability*), `QU-29` (*Close-to-Data Phenomenology*), `QU-33` (*Sharp Experimental Predictivity*), `QU-34` (*Wait for Experimental Anomalies*).
  - **Anchors Low:** `QU-17` (*Deep Elegance Reliably Guides Discovery*), `QU-21` (*Inevitable Physical Landscape*), `QU-28` (*Bayesian Non-Empirical Confirmation*), `QU-29` (*Pure Formal Structure*).

# Skin 5: Simulcapades — Computational Physics & Scientific Simulation

## 1. Domain Overview & Core Fault Lines

Computational physics and scientific simulation treat the digital computer as a virtual laboratory—bridging analytical theory and physical experiment across length and time scales unreachable by either alone. (*Note: This domain focuses strictly on scientific modeling, numerical computation, and in-silico experimentation, not Nick Bostrom's metaphysical simulation argument.*) Five deep fault lines shape the discipline:

1. **Epistemic Status — "Third Pillar of Science" vs. Automated Deduction:** Does scientific simulation generate genuinely novel, quasi-experimental knowledge alongside theory and lab work, or is it ultimately a numerical calculator constrained by whatever assumptions we typed into its equations? (Humphreys 2004; Winsberg 2010; Parker 2009).
2. **Ab Initio Realism vs. Coarse-Grained & Phenomenological Reach:** Should computational scientists insist on parameter-free quantum or direct-numerical-simulation (DNS) fidelity, or build hierarchical coarse-grained and parameterized models that actually reach macroscopic timescales and emergent complexity? (Car & Parrinello 1985; Moin & Mahesh 1998 vs. Held 2005; Marrink et al. 2007).
3. **Physics-Based Numerical Solvers vs. Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) & Neural Emulators:** Should ML serve strictly as a symmetry-constrained accelerator (neural interatomic potentials, learned subgrid closures), or can end-to-end neural operators and foundation models replace classical PDE time-steppers? (Behler & Parrinello 2007; Raissi et al. 2019; Li et al. 2021; Lam et al. 2023).
4. **Structure-Preserving Numerical Elegance vs. Brute-Force GPU Scaling:** Should simulators prioritize exact discrete conservation laws, symplectic geometry, and high-order convergence, or favor simpler, GPU-friendly kernels that ride exascale hardware throughput? (Hairer, Lubich & Wanner 2006; LeVeque 2002).
5. **Verified Community Software Infrastructure vs. Agile Research Scripts:** Should scientific codes be built as open-source, unit-tested, containerized community platforms subjected to formal Verification, Validation, and Uncertainty Quantification (V&V/UQ), or stay lean, bespoke codes that individual researchers iterate rapidly? (Oberkampf & Roy 2010; Wilson et al. 2014).

## 2. Categories

1. **Epistemology of the Virtual Laboratory** — Third pillar vs. deduction, insight vs. prediction, tuning honesty, multiscale coupling, and extrapolation trust.
2. **Modeling Paradigms & Resolution** — Continuum vs. particle vs. Monte Carlo vs. agent-based, ab initio vs. coarse-graining, turbulence closures, rare events, and quantum many-body methods.
3. **Numerical Methods & Discretization Craft** — FD/FV/FEM/Spectral trade-offs, symplectic & conservative schemes, AMR vs. uniform grids, implicit vs. explicit solvers, and floating-point precision.
4. **Machine Learning, Surrogates & Differentiable Simulation** — MLIPs & neural operators, PINNs debate, differentiable physics, hard vs. soft physical constraints, and foundation models.
5. **Verification, Validation & Uncertainty Quantification (V&V/UQ)** — Manufactured solutions, meaning of validation, UQ depth, bitwise vs. statistical reproducibility, and blind intercomparisons.
6. **HPC Architecture, Software Craft & Culture** — Language stacks, algorithms vs. hardware, flagship codes vs. bespoke scripts, open source, RSE credit, and green HPC.

## 3. Topics (34 Topics)

### Category 1: Epistemology of the Virtual Laboratory

#### `SI-01` — Epistemic Status of Scientific Simulation
- **Category:** Epistemology of the Virtual Laboratory
- **Geometry:** `triangle`
- **Poles:** `Automated Mathematical Deduction` | `Genuine Virtual Experiment` | `Distinct Third Pillar of Science`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Is a computer simulation just fancy numerical arithmetic unpacking what theory already implied, a legitimate experiment performed on a silicon substitute, or a fundamentally new third mode of science?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Automated Mathematical Deduction:* A simulation never touches physical reality; it can only reveal logical consequences of the equations and approximations programmed into it.
  - *Genuine Virtual Experiment:* Exploring nonlinear, chaotic, and multi-physics codes involves surprise, instrumentation bugs, and intervention exactly analogous to laboratory experiments.
  - *Distinct Third Pillar of Science:* Simulation fuses theoretical models, numerical craft, and empirical calibration into an autonomous epistemic pillar beside theory and experiment.
- **Primary citations:** F. Rohrlich (1990), *PSA*; P. Humphreys (2004), *Extending Ourselves*; W. S. Parker (2009), *Synthese*; E. Winsberg (2010), *Science in the Age of Computer Simulation*.

#### `SI-02` — Prediction Fidelity vs. Conceptual Understanding
- **Category:** Epistemology of the Virtual Laboratory
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Minimal Models for Insight` | `Hierarchical Model Ladder` | `High-Fidelity Quantitative Prediction`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** As complex simulations (e.g., Earth-system or galaxy-formation models) become almost as opaque as nature itself, should we strip them down for understanding or push resolution ever higher?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Minimal Models for Insight:* "The purpose of computing is insight, not numbers" (Hamming); a giant black-box simulator that reproduces reality without explaining *why* misses the point of physics.
  - *Hierarchical Model Ladder:* Pair realistic flagship simulations with a ladder of simplified idealized models so every emergent phenomenon can be traced to its core mechanism (Held).
  - *High-Fidelity Quantitative Prediction:* Real systems are irreducibly coupled; oversimplified toy models mislead, whereas high-resolution realism captures critical nonlinear feedbacks accurately.
- **Primary citations:** R. W. Hamming (1962), *Numerical Methods for Scientists and Engineers*; I. M. Held (2005), "The Gap Between Simulation and Understanding in Climate Modeling," *Bull. Am. Meteorol. Soc.*

#### `SI-03` — Subgrid Tuning & Parameterization Honesty
- **Category:** Epistemology of the Virtual Laboratory
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Reject Ad-Hoc Tuning—Resolve Physics` | `Transparent Documented Calibration` | `Pragmatic Tuning for System Skill`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Unresolved subgrid physics (clouds, star formation, turbulence viscosity) requires tunable closure parameters; is tuning necessary craft or scientific fudge-factoring?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Reject Ad-Hoc Tuning—Resolve Physics:* Tuning free closure constants masks structural errors with compensating biases; invest compute in resolving the underlying physics directly.
  - *Transparent Documented Calibration:* Tuning is unavoidable in finite-resolution models, so treat it as an explicit, published, reproducible inverse problem with held-out validation metrics.
  - *Pragmatic Tuning for System Skill:* Forecasting real-world systems requires calibrating effective subgrid closures to observations—judge models by out-of-sample skill, not purism.
- **Primary citations:** F. Hourdin et al. (2017), "The Art and Science of Climate Model Tuning," *Bull. Am. Meteorol. Soc.*; E. Winsberg (2010).

#### `SI-04` — Multiscale Coupling Architecture
- **Category:** Epistemology of the Virtual Laboratory
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Concurrent Handshaking (QM/MM, Coupled)` | `Sequential Parameter Passing` | `Autonomous Single-Scale Effective Models`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** When phenomena span quantum electrons to continuum mechanics, how should simulators bridge the scales?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Concurrent Handshaking (QM/MM, Coupled):* Dynamically couple fine and coarse solvers on the fly where localized reactive or crack-tip physics feeds back into the continuum.
  - *Sequential Parameter Passing:* Run accurate microscale simulations offline to extract equations of state, transport coefficients, or force fields for clean macroscale codes.
  - *Autonomous Single-Scale Effective Models:* Scale separation usually holds; clean renormalization-group effective models avoid the notorious artifact boundaries of hybrid handshaking zones.
- **Primary citations:** A. Warshel & M. Levitt (1976), *J. Mol. Biol.* (QM/MM); W. E & B. Engquist (2003), "The Heterogeneous Multiscale Methods," *Commun. Math. Sci.*

#### `SI-05` — Trusting Simulations in Unobserved Regimes
- **Category:** Epistemology of the Virtual Laboratory
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Never Trust Beyond Validated Data` | `Physics-Bounded Extrapolation with UQ` | `First-Principles Laws Guarantee Reach`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Many premier simulations—planetary cores, century-scale climate, nuclear stockpiles, black-hole mergers—predict regimes where direct experiments are impossible; how far does trust extend?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Never Trust Beyond Validated Data:* Validation only tests the interpolation envelope; extrapolating any numerical model into unmeasured regimes is inherently fragile (Oreskes et al.).
  - *Physics-Bounded Extrapolation with UQ:* Extrapolation is credible when anchored to exact conservation laws, hierarchical sub-component tests, and honest structural-discrepancy uncertainty bounds.
  - *First-Principles Laws Guarantee Reach:* General relativity, Maxwell's equations, and quantum mechanics hold universally—numerically converged solutions of fundamental equations predict unobserved regimes reliably.
- **Primary citations:** N. Oreskes, K. Shrader-Frechette & K. Belitz (1994), "Verification, Validation, and Confirmation of Numerical Models in the Earth Sciences," *Science*; W. L. Oberkampf & C. J. Roy (2010).

### Category 2: Modeling Paradigms & Resolution

#### `SI-06` — Primary Simulation Worldview
- **Category:** Modeling Paradigms & Resolution
- **Geometry:** `quad-compass`
- **Poles:** `Continuum PDE Field Solvers` | `Deterministic Particle & Molecular Dynamics` | `Stochastic Monte Carlo & Lattice` | `Discrete-Event & Agent-Based`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Which computational representation of physical and complex systems feels most natural and authoritative to you?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Continuum PDE Field Solvers:* Fluids, plasmas, elastodynamics, and curved spacetimes are cleanest as smooth partial differential equations discretized on meshes.
  - *Deterministic Particle & Molecular Dynamics:* Integrating Newton's or Hamilton's equations for millions of interacting particles reveals real-time microscopic dynamics directly.
  - *Stochastic Monte Carlo & Lattice:* Path integrals, equilibrium phase transitions, and non-perturbative QCD demand importance sampling over vast configuration spaces.
  - *Discrete-Event & Agent-Based:* Heterogeneous, adaptive, far-from-equilibrium systems emerge naturally from local interaction rules among discrete autonomous entities.
- **Primary citations:** N. Metropolis et al. (1953), *J. Chem. Phys.*; B. J. Alder & T. E. Wainwright (1957), *J. Chem. Phys.*; K. G. Wilson (1974), *Phys. Rev. D*; J. M. Epstein & R. Axtell (1996).

#### `SI-07` — Ab Initio Fidelity vs. Coarse-Grained Reach
- **Category:** Modeling Paradigms & Resolution
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Parameter-Free Ab Initio First` | `Bottom-Up Systematic Coarse-Graining` | `Top-Down Phenomenological Reach`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Should atomistic and condensed-matter simulation prioritize quantum-accurate electrons over short picoseconds, or lump atoms into coarse beads to watch milliseconds of self-assembly?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Parameter-Free Ab Initio First:* Empirical force fields break down whenever bonds form, charge transfers, or environments shift; ab initio electronic structure (AIMD/QMC) is the only honest baseline.
  - *Bottom-Up Systematic Coarse-Graining:* Derive reduced potentials systematically via force-matching or relative entropy from ab initio data so fidelity and scale reinforce each other.
  - *Top-Down Phenomenological Reach:* Calibrating coarse beads (like MARTINI) to thermodynamic partitioning lets us actually simulate membranes, viruses, and phase separation at realistic scales.
- **Primary citations:** R. Car & M. Parrinello (1985), *Phys. Rev. Lett.*; S. J. Marrink et al. (2007), *J. Phys. Chem. B*; W. G. Noid (2013), *J. Chem. Phys.*

#### `SI-08` — Turbulence & Nonlinear Cascade Strategy
- **Category:** Modeling Paradigms & Resolution
- **Geometry:** `triangle`
- **Poles:** `Direct Numerical Simulation (DNS)` | `Large-Eddy Simulation (LES)` | `Reynolds-Averaged Closures (RANS)`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Facing Kolmogorov's brutal $Re^{9/4}$ scaling of degrees of freedom in turbulent flows, where do you draw the line between resolving eddies and modeling them?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Direct Numerical Simulation (DNS):* Resolve all the way down to the Kolmogorov dissipation scale with zero turbulence-model bias—even if Reynolds numbers stay modest.
  - *Large-Eddy Simulation (LES):* Explicitly simulate the problem-dependent energy-containing eddies and model only the more universal subgrid inertial-dissipation tail.
  - *Reynolds-Averaged Closures (RANS):* Engineering design sweeps across thousands of geometries demand fast, reliable statistical closures rather than hero runs taking months.
- **Primary citations:** P. Moin & K. Mahesh (1998), "Direct Numerical Simulation: A Tool in Turbulence Research," *Annu. Rev. Fluid Mech.*; S. B. Pope (2000), *Turbulent Flows*.

#### `SI-09` — Rare Events & Conformational Sampling
- **Category:** Modeling Paradigms & Resolution
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Brute-Force Long Unbiased Trajectories` | `Enhanced Biasing (Metadynamics / Umbrella)` | `Path Sampling & Markov State Models`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** When nucleation, protein folding, or reaction barriers take microseconds to seconds while timesteps are femtoseconds, how should simulators conquer rare events?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Brute-Force Long Unbiased Trajectories:* Adding biasing potentials distorts pathways and misses hidden collective variables; specialized ultra-fast hardware running unbiased dynamics tells the unvarnished truth (Anton ethos).
  - *Enhanced Biasing (Metadynamics / Umbrella):* Smart collective variables and history-dependent biases reconstruct free-energy landscapes orders of magnitude faster than brute force.
  - *Path Sampling & Markov State Models:* Adaptive ensembles of short unbiased trajectories stitched into transition networks or TPS capture true kinetics without artificial force bias.
- **Primary citations:** D. E. Shaw et al. (2008), *Commun. ACM* (Anton); A. Laio & M. Parrinello (2002), *PNAS* (Metadynamics); P. G. Bolhuis et al. (2002), *Annu. Rev. Phys. Chem.* (TPS).

#### `SI-10` — Quantum Many-Body Simulation Frontier
- **Category:** Modeling Paradigms & Resolution
- **Geometry:** `triangle`
- **Poles:** `Quantum Monte Carlo & Lattice QFT` | `Tensor Networks (DMRG / PEPS)` | `Analog & Digital Quantum Simulators`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Facing the exponential Hilbert-space wall and fermion sign problem, which computational engine will crack strongly correlated quantum matter?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Quantum Monte Carlo & Lattice QFT:* Stochastic sampling and clever basis/constraint tricks (AFQMC, fixed-node DMC, Lattice QCD) deliver unmatched 3D precision across physics.
  - *Tensor Networks (DMRG / PEPS):* Area-law entanglement structure lets matrix-product states and projected entangled-pair states defeat the sign problem deterministically.
  - *Analog & Digital Quantum Simulators:* As Feynman foresaw, classical bits hit an exponential wall for real-time quantum dynamics; programmable neutral atoms, ions, and qubits are the ultimate simulator.
- **Primary citations:** R. P. Feynman (1982), "Simulating Physics with Computers," *Int. J. Theor. Phys.*; S. R. White (1992), *Phys. Rev. Lett.* (DMRG); M. Troyer & U.-J. Wiese (2005), *Phys. Rev. Lett.* (sign problem).

#### `SI-11` — Single Hero Run vs. Large Statistical Ensembles
- **Category:** Modeling Paradigms & Resolution
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `One Ultra-High-Resolution Hero Run` | `Resolution-Ensemble Compromise` | `Massive Lower-Res Probabilistic Ensembles`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Given a fixed supercomputer allocation for chaotic dynamics (weather, astrophysics, plasma), should you spend it on one breathtaking ultra-fine run or 500 coarser ensemble members?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *One Ultra-High-Resolution Hero Run:* Crossing a critical physical resolution threshold (resolving convection, shocks, or magneto-rotational instability) changes the qualitative physics completely.
  - *Resolution-Ensemble Compromise:* Run a small ensemble at high enough resolution to separate physical regime shifts from chaotic initial-condition spread.
  - *Massive Lower-Res Probabilistic Ensembles:* In chaotic systems, one deterministic trajectory is statistically meaningless; large ensembles with stochastic physics quantify risk and tail events honestly.
- **Primary citations:** E. N. Lorenz (1963), *J. Atmos. Sci.*; T. N. Palmer (2019), "Stochastic Weather and Climate Models," *Nat. Rev. Phys.*

### Category 3: Numerical Methods & Discretization Craft

#### `SI-12` — Spatial Discretization Preference
- **Category:** Numerical Methods & Discretization Craft
- **Geometry:** `diamond`
- **Poles:** `Finite Differences (FD)` | `Conservative Finite Volumes (FV)` | `Finite Elements & Discontinuous Galerkin` | `Spectral & Meshfree Particle Methods`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** When discretizing continuum physics onto a computer, which numerical family is your gold standard?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Finite Differences (FD):* Simple, fast, cache-friendly, and trivially extensible to high-order stencils on structured grids.
  - *Conservative Finite Volumes (FV):* Guarantees exact discrete conservation of mass, momentum, and energy across shocks and discontinuities via flux-balanced cells.
  - *Finite Elements & Discontinuous Galerkin:* Handles intricate engineering geometries with rigorous weak-form error bounds and arbitrary polynomial order ($hp$-adaptivity).
  - *Spectral & Meshfree Particle Methods:* Spectral methods achieve exponential convergence on smooth domains, while SPH/meshfree methods track violent free surfaces and fragmentation effortlessly.
- **Primary citations:** R. J. LeVeque (2002), *Finite Volume Methods for Hyperbolic Problems*; L. N. Trefethen (2000), *Spectral Methods in MATLAB*; J. S. Hesthaven & T. Warburton (2008), *Nodal Discontinuous Galerkin Methods*.

#### `SI-13` — Structure-Preserving Geometric Integration
- **Category:** Numerical Methods & Discretization Craft
- **Geometry:** `monopole-slider`
- **Poles:** `Insistence on Symplectic & Exact-Conservation Integrators`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Should numerical algorithms strictly preserve the continuous system's geometric invariants (symplectic phase-space volume, $\nabla\cdot\mathbf{B}=0$, exact energy/momentum) rather than merely shrinking truncation error?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Insistence on Symplectic & Exact-Conservation Integrators:* Over millions of orbits or timesteps, non-symplectic solvers drift catastrophically; preserving discrete geometry keeps qualitative long-term physics faithful.
- **Primary citations:** E. Hairer, C. Lubich & G. Wanner (2006), *Geometric Numerical Integration*; C. R. Evans & J. F. Hawley (1988), *ApJ* (constrained transport).

#### `SI-14` — Adaptive Mesh Refinement (AMR) vs. Uniform Grids
- **Category:** Numerical Methods & Discretization Craft
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Clean Uniform Structured Grids` | `Static Nested / Unstructured Meshes` | `Dynamic Adaptive Mesh Refinement`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Should simulations concentrate resolution dynamically where gradients explode, or avoid coarse-fine reflection artifacts and load-imbalance headaches with uniform grids?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Clean Uniform Structured Grids:* Uniform grids eliminate spurious wave reflections at refinement boundaries, vectorize effortlessly on GPUs, and have crystal-clear numerical dissipation.
  - *Static Nested / Unstructured Meshes:* Pre-adapted static grids capture known geometric bottlenecks cleanly without dynamic regridding overhead or shifting truncation error.
  - *Dynamic Adaptive Mesh Refinement:* Multi-decade spatial dynamic range (star formation, shock fronts, black-hole inspiral) is computationally impossible without dynamic AMR.
- **Primary citations:** M. J. Berger & J. Oliger (1984), *J. Comput. Phys.*; M. J. Berger & P. Colella (1989), *J. Comput. Phys.*

#### `SI-15` — Explicit Scalability vs. Implicit Stiff Solvers
- **Category:** Numerical Methods & Discretization Craft
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Explicit Local Time-Stepping` | `IMEX Operator Splitting` | `Fully Implicit Newton–Krylov`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** When fast waves or stiff chemical/diffusive timescales restrict the CFL condition, do you take millions of tiny cheap explicit steps or invert global coupled systems implicitly?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Explicit Local Time-Stepping:* Simple stencil updates have near-ideal GPU arithmetic intensity and parallel scalability with zero fragile nonlinear-solver stalls.
  - *IMEX Operator Splitting:* Treat stiff local diffusion or reactions implicitly while stepping advection explicitly—getting the best of both stability and speed.
  - *Fully Implicit Newton–Krylov:* Coupled multi-physics (fusion MHD, mantle convection, stiff combustion) require preconditioned fully implicit solvers to advance on the physical timescale of interest.
- **Primary citations:** U. M. Ascher, S. J. Ruuth & R. J. Spiteri (1997), *Appl. Numer. Math.* (IMEX); D. A. Knoll & D. E. Keyes (2004), *J. Comput. Phys.* (JFNK).

#### `SI-16` — Floating-Point Precision Discipline
- **Category:** Numerical Methods & Discretization Craft
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Strict FP64 (or Higher) Everywhere` | `Mixed Precision with FP64 Accumulation` | `Embrace Low-Precision Tensor Cores`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Modern AI-driven GPUs offer massive speedups in FP16/BF16/FP32 at the expense of FP64 throughput; how aggressively should scientific codes lower precision?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Strict FP64 (or Higher) Everywhere:* Round-off accumulation, ill-conditioned linear solves, and catastrophic cancellation silently corrupt physics; never gamble scientific accuracy for cheap FLOPs.
  - *Mixed Precision with FP64 Accumulation:* Run iterative refinement, preconditioners, and pairwise forces in lower precision while keeping global sums and outer residuals strictly FP64.
  - *Embrace Low-Precision Tensor Cores:* Most simulations have discretization or model error orders of magnitude larger than $10^{-7}$; low-precision compute buys vastly higher resolution or bigger ensembles.
- **Primary citations:** N. J. Higham (2002), *Accuracy and Stability of Numerical Algorithms*; A. Abdelfattah et al. (2021), "A Survey of Numerical Linear Algebra Methods Utilizing Mixed-Precision Arithmetic," *IJHPCA*.

---

### Category 4: Machine Learning, Surrogates & Differentiable Simulation

#### `SI-17` — ML Surrogates vs. Classical Physics Solvers
- **Category:** Machine Learning, Surrogates & Differentiable Simulation
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Pure First-Principles Solvers Only` | `Hybrid ML-Accelerated Physics` | `End-to-End Neural Operators & Emulators`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** What is the rightful place of deep learning in computational physics—strictly banned from production physics, embedded inside classical solvers, or replacing classical PDE steppers outright?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Pure First-Principles Solvers Only:* Neural surrogates fail silently outside training distributions and lack convergence theorems; keep production physics purely equation-based.
  - *Hybrid ML-Accelerated Physics:* Combine exact continuum/molecular integrators with ML interatomic potentials (MLIPs) or learned subgrid closures to gain $10^4\times$ speedups with physical guardrails.
  - *End-to-End Neural Operators & Emulators:* Learned weather models (GraphCast) and Fourier neural operators already match or beat classical solvers at a fraction of the energy and wall-clock time.
- **Primary citations:** J. Behler & M. Parrinello (2007), *Phys. Rev. Lett.*; Z. Li et al. (2021), *ICLR* (Fourier Neural Operator); R. Lam et al. (2023), "Learning Skillful Medium-Range Global Weather Forecasting," *Science*.

#### `SI-18` — Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs): Promise or Hype?
- **Category:** Machine Learning, Surrogates & Differentiable Simulation
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Overhyped vs. Classical FEM/FV` | `Strong for Inverse & Noisy Data Assimilation` | `Transformative for High-Dimensional PDEs`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Embedding PDE residuals into neural-network loss functions (PINNs) exploded across computational literature—do they outperform classical solvers or solve a different problem?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Overhyped vs. Classical FEM/FV:* On forward PDE benchmarks, classical FEM/FV/spectral methods beat PINNs by orders of magnitude in accuracy, speed, and reliability.
  - *Strong for Inverse & Noisy Data Assimilation:* PINNs shine not as forward PDE replacements, but when fusing sparse, noisy experimental measurements with partial physical equations to infer hidden parameters.
  - *Transformative for High-Dimensional PDEs:* Mesh-free neural representations break the curse of dimensionality in high-dimensional Fokker–Planck, Hamilton–Jacobi, and Schrödinger equations.
- **Primary citations:** M. Raissi, P. Perdikaris & G. E. Karniadakis (2019), *J. Comput. Phys.*; T. G. Grossmann et al. (2024), *IMA J. Appl. Math.* (PINN vs. FEM systematic comparison).

#### `SI-19` — Differentiable Physics & Automatic Differentiation
- **Category:** Machine Learning, Surrogates & Differentiable Simulation
- **Geometry:** `monopole-slider`
- **Poles:** `Priority of End-to-End Differentiable Simulators`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Should the next generation of physics simulators be written natively in AD-capable frameworks (JAX, PyTorch, Enzyme, Zygote) so exact gradients flow through entire trajectories?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Priority of End-to-End Differentiable Simulators:* Reverse-mode adjoints through a simulator turn inverse design, sensitivity analysis, optimal control, and ML-closure training from intractable black-box searches into fast gradient descent.
- **Primary citations:** A. Griewank & A. Walther (2008), *Evaluating Derivatives*; S. S. Schoenholz & E. D. Cubuk (2020), *NeurIPS* (JAX MD); D. Kochkov et al. (2021), *PNAS*.

#### `SI-20` — Hard Symmetry Constraints vs. Soft Scale-Driven Learning in SciML
- **Category:** Machine Learning, Surrogates & Differentiable Simulation
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Hard Equivariance & Exact Conservation` | `Pragmatic Inductive Biases` | `Bitter-Lesson Scale Learns Symmetries Softly`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Should scientific neural architectures hard-code $E(3)$ equivariance, gauge invariance, and exact conservation laws, or let large, simple unconstrained architectures learn them from data?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Hard Equivariance & Exact Conservation:* Baking rotational/translational symmetry and energy-conservation into network layers dramatically boosts data efficiency and physical stability (NequIP/MACE).
  - *Pragmatic Inductive Biases:* Enforce cheap, high-leverage physical constraints while avoiding overly rigid specialized layers that bottleneck GPU throughput.
  - *Bitter-Lesson Scale Learns Symmetries Softly:* History in AI shows general scalable architectures with data augmentation and huge compute eventually outstrip hand-crafted inductive constraints.
- **Primary citations:** S. Batzner et al. (2022), *Nat. Commun.* (NequIP); R. Sutton (2019), "The Bitter Lesson" (applied to SciML debates on equivariant vs. unconstrained transformers).

#### `SI-21` — AlphaFold & Scientific Foundation Models
- **Category:** Machine Learning, Surrogates & Differentiable Simulation
- **Geometry:** `trilinear-line`
- **Poles:** `Black-Box Pattern Matcher Missing Physics` | `Powerful Catalyst Needing Physical Integration` | `New Blueprint for Computational Science`
- **Hovertip (`issue`):** Does AlphaFold's triumph over physics-based molecular-dynamics protein folding herald the future of all simulation science, or represent a special data-rich anomaly?
- **Per-pole hovertips:**
  - *Black-Box Pattern Matcher Missing Physics:* AlphaFold memorizes evolutionary co-variation to predict static crystal structures without explaining folding pathways, dynamics, or novel non-biological chemistry.
  - *Powerful Catalyst Needing Physical Integration:* Foundation models excel at jumping straight to good initial hypotheses, which physics-based simulations then refine for dynamics, free energies, and out-of-distribution states.
  - *New Blueprint for Computational Science:* Pretraining multimodal foundation models across massive simulated and experimental datasets will unify materials, weather, plasma, and molecular design.
- **Primary citations:** J. Jumper et al. (2021), "Highly Accurate Protein Structure Prediction with AlphaFold," *Nature*; D. Baker & D. Hassabis/J. Jumper (2024), Nobel Prize in Chemistry paradigm.
